Shape: (n_samples, n_channels)\ndt = float(data['dt'])   # Sampling interval\nchannels = str(data['channels'])  # Channel name(s)\n\n# Calculate time axis\nn_samples = waveform.shape[0]\ntime = np.arange(n_samples) * dt  # Time in seconds\n```\n\n### Processing All Files in Directory\n```python\nimport os\nimport numpy as np\n\ndata_dir = '/root/data/'\ntraces = []\n\nfor filename in os.listdir(data_dir):\n    if filename.endswith('.npz'):\n        filepath = os.path.join(data_dir, filename)\n        data = np.load(filepath)\n        traces.append({\n            'filename': filename,\n            'waveform': data['data'],\n            'dt': float(data['dt']),\n            'channels': str(data['channels'])\n        })\n```\n\n### Data Normalization\n```python\n# Z-score normalization (per channel)\ndef normalize_trace(waveform):\n    normalized = np.zeros_like(waveform, dtype=float)\n    for ch in range(waveform.shape[1]):\n        mean = np.mean(waveform[:, ch])\n        std = np.std(waveform[:, ch])\n        if std > 0:\n            normalized[:, ch] = (waveform[:, ch] - mean) / std\n    return normalized\n\n# Demeaning (remove DC offset)\ndef demean_trace(waveform):\n    return waveform - np.mean(waveform, axis=0, keepdims=True)\n```\n\n### Channel Selection\n```python\n# If multiple channels available, select specific one\nchannels_str = str(data['channels'])\nchannel_list = channels_str.split(',')\nprint(f\"Available channels: {channel_list}\")\n\n# Use vertical (Z) channel if available\nif 'Z' in channel_list:\n    z_idx = channel_list.index('Z')\n    z_data = data['data'][:, z_idx]\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n1. Always check data shape and channels before processing\n2. Handle both single and multi-channel data gracefully\n3. Preserve original sampling rate information (dt)\n4. Be aware of data units and ranges\n5. Check for NaN or infinite values before processing\n\n## Common Issues\n- **Channel naming inconsistency**: Some files use \"Z\" vs \"DPZ\" for vertical component\n- **Byte ordering**: Ensure correct endianness when loading\n- **Missing fields**: Not all optional fields are present in every file\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":5,"cache_creation_input_tokens":125,"cache_read_input_tokens":20950,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":125,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":18},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"09e6d993-23e9-426b-836d-7610873e9d50","uuid":"de7c0229-09c8-482f-94d9-debc8b98bdf4"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_016ka78ZT42o2wnhXzzJxGhW","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01JmboDH5o414zFtNaZMhimk","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/environment/skills/phase-picking-algorithms.md","content":"# Seismic Phase Picking Algorithms\n\n## Overview\nPhase picking is the process of identifying the arrival times of seismic waves (P and S waves) in waveform data. This skill covers practical algorithms used in seismology for automatic phase detection.\n\n## Installation\nRequired packages:\n```bash\npip install numpy scipy scikit-learn obspy\n```\n\n## Physical Properties of P and S Waves\n\n### P Waves (Primary/Compressional)\n- Fastest seismic waves\n- Arrive first at the station\n- Typically show sharp onset with amplitude increase\n- Lower frequency content\n- Travel faster through rock (~6 km/s)\n\n### S Waves (Secondary/Shear)\n- Slower than P waves\n- Arrive later, after P waves\n- Larger amplitudes than P waves\n- Higher frequency content\n- Travel slower (~3.5 km/s)\n\n## Common Phase Picking Methods\n\n### 1. STA/LTA Method (Short-Term Average / Long-Term Average)\nBest for: Automatic, real-time picking\n\n```python\nimport numpy as np\n\ndef sta_lta_picker(waveform, dt, sta_window=1.0, lta_window=10.0, threshold=2.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    STA/LTA phase picker\n\n    Args:\n        waveform: 1D array of seismic data\n        dt: Sampling interval in seconds\n        sta_window: Short-term window length in seconds\n        lta_window: Long-term window length in seconds\n        threshold: STA/LTA ratio threshold for detection\n\n    Returns:\n        onset_index: Index of phase arrival (or None if not found)\n    \"\"\"\n    n_sta = int(sta_window / dt)\n    n_lta = int(lta_window / dt)\n\n    # Calculate squared amplitudes for energy-based detection\n    energy = waveform ** 2\n\n    # Compute STA and LTA\n    sta = np.convolve(energy, np.ones(n_sta) / n_sta, mode='same')\n    lta = np.convolve(energy, np.ones(n_lta) / n_lta, mode='same')\n\n    # Avoid division by zero\n    lta[lta == 0] = 1\n\n    # Compute ratio\n    ratio = sta / lta\n\n    # Find first exceedance\n    onset_indices = np.where(ratio > threshold)[0]\n\n    if len(onset_indices) > 0:\n        return onset_indices[0]\n    return None\n```\n\n### 2. Amplitude-Based Energy Method\nBest for: Strong signal, good SNR\n\n```python\ndef energy_picker(waveform, dt, window_size=1.0, percentile=90):\n    \"\"\"\n    Energy-based picker using envelope and percentile\n\n    Args:\n        waveform: 1D array of seismic data\n        dt: Sampling interval\n        window_size: Smoothing window in seconds\n        percentile: Energy threshold percentile (0-100)\n\n    Returns:\n        onset_index: Index of significant energy increase\n    \"\"\"\n    # Compute envelope (absolute value)\n    envelope = np.abs(waveform)\n\n    # Smooth envelope\n    window = int(window_size / dt)\n    kernel = np.ones(window) / window\n    smooth_envelope = np.convolve(envelope, kernel, mode='same')\n\n    # Compute energy threshold\n    threshold = np.percentile(smooth_envelope, percentile)\n\n    # Find first crossing\n    onset_indices = np.where(smooth_envelope > threshold)[0]\n\n    if len(onset_indices) > 0:\n        return onset_indices[0]\n    return None\n```\n\n### 3. Frequency-Domain Method (Spectrogram-based)\nBest for: Identifying S waves (higher frequencies)\n\n```python\nfrom scipy import signal\nimport numpy as np\n\ndef spectrogram_picker(waveform, dt, freq_range=(5, 20), threshold_percentile=85):\n    \"\"\"\n    Picker based on spectrogram energy in specific frequency band\n\n    Args:\n        waveform: 1D array\n        dt: Sampling interval\n        freq_range: Tuple (min_freq, max_freq) in Hz\n        threshold_percentile: Energy threshold percentile\n\n    Returns:\n        onset_index: Index where energy in freq_range increases\n    \"\"\"\n    # Compute spectrogram\n    f, t, Sxx = signal.spectrogram(waveform, fs=1/dt, nperseg=256)\n\n    # Select frequency range\n    freq_mask = (f >= freq_range[0]) & (f <= freq_range[1])\n    power_in_band = np.sum(Sxx[freq_mask, :], axis=0)\n\n    # Smooth\n    smoothed = signal.medfilt(power_in_band, kernel_size=5)\n\n    # Threshold\n    threshold = np.percentile(smoothed, threshold_percentile)\n    onset_indices = np.where(smoothed > threshold)[0]\n\n    if len(onset_indices) > 0:\n        return onset_indices[0]\n    return None\n```\n\n### 4. Multi-Channel Stacking (For 3-Component Data)\nBest for: Improving SNR across channels\n\n```python\ndef multi_channel_picker(waveform_3c, dt, method='energy'):\n    \"\"\"\n    Combine picks from all 3 channels\n\n    Args:\n        waveform_3c: 2D array (n_samples, 3) for Z, N, E components\n\n    Returns:\n        onset_index: Consensus pick\n    \"\"\"\n    # Normalize each channel\n    normalized = np.zeros_like(waveform_3c, dtype=float)\n    for i in range(3):\n        ch_data = waveform_3c[:, i]\n        normalized[:, i] = (ch_data - np.mean(ch_data)) / np.std(ch_data)\n\n    # Compute quadratic sum (combined energy)\n    combined_energy = np.sum(normalized ** 2, axis=1)\n\n    # Apply picker on combined energy\n    # Use energy_picker or sta_lta_picker on combined_energy\n    return combined_energy\n```\n\n## Wave Identification Strategy\n\n### P Wave Picking\n1. Start from early in the trace (near origin)\n2. Look for first significant amplitude increase\n3. P waves appear within ~5-30 seconds for local-to-regional distances\n4. Use lower frequency window (1-5 Hz) for robust picking\n\n### S Wave Picking\n1. Start after P wave arrival\n2. Look for strong amplitude increase (higher energy than P)\n3. S waves typically 1.73× farther from origin than P (v_p/v_s ≈ 1.73)\n4. Use higher frequency window (5-20 Hz) where S has more energy\n5. S waves show larger amplitude variations on horizontal components\n\n## Best Practices\n1. **Preprocessing**: Always demean and normalize traces before picking\n2. **Multi-parameter approach**: Use multiple methods and combine results\n3. **Parameter tuning**: Adjust window sizes and thresholds based on data characteristics\n4. **Validation**: Compare automatic picks with manual review on subset\n5. **Noise handling**: Use robust statistics (median) instead of mean\n6. **Channel weighting**: Weight vertical component more for P, horizontal for S\n\n## Performance Optimization\n- Pre-compute energy/envelope to avoid redundant calculations\n- Vectorize operations using NumPy\n- Consider early stopping once phase is found\n- Cache intermediate results for multi-method approaches\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":3,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1160,"cache_read_input_tokens":21075,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1160,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"09e6d993-23e9-426b-836d-7610873e9d50","uuid":"fad29c28-a2b1-491f-934a-c353691e782a"}

Filtering (Noise Reduction)\n\n#### Butterworth Filter\nBest for removing low-frequency noise while preserving phase information\n\n```python\nfrom scipy import signal\nimport numpy as np\n\ndef bandpass_filter(data, dt, lowcut, highcut, order=4):\n    \"\"\"\n    Butterworth bandpass filter\n\n    Args:\n        data: Input signal (1D or 2D array)\n        dt: Sampling interval in seconds\n        lowcut: Low cutoff frequency in Hz\n        highcut: High cutoff frequency in Hz\n        order: Filter order\n\n    Returns:\n        filtered: Filtered signal\n    \"\"\"\n    nyquist = 1 / (2 * dt)\n    low = lowcut / nyquist\n    high = highcut / nyquist\n\n    # Ensure valid range\n    low = max(0.001, min(low, 0.99))\n    high = max(low + 0.001, min(high, 0.99))\n\n    b, a = signal.butter(order, [low, high], btype='band')\n\n    if data.ndim == 1:\n        return signal.filtfilt(b, a, data)\n    else:\n        # Apply to each channel\n        filtered = np.zeros_like(data, dtype=float)\n        for i in range(data.shape[1]):\n            filtered[:, i] = signal.filtfilt(b, a, data[:, i])\n        return filtered\n```\n\n#### High-Pass Filter\nFor removing very low frequency components and drift\n\n```python\ndef highpass_filter(data, dt, cutoff, order=4):\n    \"\"\"High-pass filter for drift removal\"\"\"\n    nyquist = 1 / (2 * dt)\n    norm_cutoff = min(cutoff / nyquist, 0.99)\n    b, a = signal.butter(order, norm_cutoff, btype='high')\n\n    if data.ndim == 1:\n        return signal.filtfilt(b, a, data)\n    else:\n        filtered = np.zeros_like(data, dtype=float)\n        for i in range(data.shape[1]):\n            filtered[:, i] = signal.filtfilt(b, a, data[:, i])\n        return filtered\n```\n\n### 2. Envelope Computation (Hilbert Transform)\n\n```python\nfrom scipy import signal\nimport numpy as np\n\ndef compute_envelope(data):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute envelope using Hilbert transform\n\n    Analytic signal: z(t) = x(t) + j*H[x(t)]\n    Envelope (magnitude): A(t) = sqrt(x(t)^2 + H[x(t)]^2)\n\n    Args:\n        data: 1D or 2D array\n\n    Returns:\n        envelope: Magnitude of analytic signal\n    \"\"\"\n    if data.ndim == 1:\n        analytic_signal = signal.hilbert(data)\n        return np.abs(analytic_signal)\n    else:\n        envelope = np.zeros_like(data, dtype=float)\n        for i in range(data.shape[1]):\n            analytic_signal = signal.hilbert(data[:, i])\n            envelope[:, i] = np.abs(analytic_signal)\n        return envelope\n```\n\n### 3. Time-Frequency Analysis\n\n#### Short-Time Fourier Transform (STFT)\n```python\ndef compute_stft(data, dt, nperseg=256):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute STFT spectrogram\n\n    Args:\n        data: 1D signal\n        dt: Sampling interval\n        nperseg: Segment length for FFT\n\n    Returns:\n        frequencies: Frequency array\n        times: Time array\n        spectrogram: Power spectrogram (2D)\n    \"\"\"\n    fs = 1 / dt\n    frequencies, times, spectrogram = signal.spectrogram(\n        data, fs=fs, nperseg=nperseg, noverlap=nperseg//2\n    )\n    return frequencies, times, spectrogram\n```\n\n#### Continuous Wavelet Transform (CWT)\nBetter for transient detection (phase onsets)\n\n```python\nfrom scipy import signal\nimport numpy as np\n\ndef compute_cwt(data, dt, scales=None, wavelet='morlet'):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute CWT for transient detection\n\n    Args:\n        data: 1D signal\n        dt: Sampling interval\n        scales: Scales to analyze (default: auto)\n        wavelet: Wavelet type ('morlet', 'mexh', etc.)\n\n    Returns:\n        coefficients: CWT coefficients\n        frequencies: Corresponding frequencies\n    \"\"\"\n    fs = 1 / dt\n    if scales is None:\n        scales = np.arange(1, 128)\n\n    coefficients = signal.cwt(data, signal.morlet2, scales)\n    frequencies = scales / (dt * len(data))\n\n    return coefficients, frequencies\n```\n\n### 4. Data Preprocessing\n\n#### Complete Preprocessing Pipeline\n```python\ndef preprocess_trace(waveform, dt, highpass_freq=0.5, lowpass_freq=20):\n    \"\"\"\n    Complete preprocessing: denoise, filter, normalize\n\n    Args:\n        waveform: 2D array (n_samples, n_channels)\n        dt: Sampling interval\n        highpass_freq: High-pass cutoff in Hz\n        lowpass_freq: Low-pass cutoff in Hz\n\n    Returns:\n        processed: Preprocessed waveform\n    \"\"\"\n    # 1. Demean\n    detrended = waveform - np.mean(waveform, axis=0, keepdims=True)\n\n    # 2. Remove linear trend\n    for i in range(detrended.shape[1]):\n        coeffs = np.polyfit(np.arange(len(detrended)), detrended[:, i], 1)\n        trend = np.polyval(coeffs, np.arange(len(detrended)))\n        detrended[:, i] -= trend\n\n    # 3. Bandpass filter\n    filtered = bandpass_filter(detrended, dt, highpass_freq, lowpass_freq)\n\n    # 4. Normalize per channel\n    normalized = np.zeros_like(filtered, dtype=float)\n    for i in range(filtered.shape[1]):\n        std = np.std(filtered[:, i])\n        if std > 0:\n            normalized[:, i] = filtered[:, i] / std\n\n    return normalized\n```\n\n## Frequency Band Selection by Phase Type\n\n### For P Wave Detection\n- **Primary band**: 1-5 Hz (lower frequencies, first arrival)\n- **Complementary**: 0.5-2 Hz for very regional distances\n- Reasoning: P waves have lower frequency content, reduces S wave interference\n\n### For S Wave Detection\n- **Primary band**: 5-20 Hz (higher frequencies, larger amplitudes)\n- **Complementary**: 3-10 Hz for regional distances\n- Reasoning: S waves contain more energy in higher frequencies\n\n### For Joint Analysis\n- **Broad band**: 0.5-20 Hz for overall energy detection\n- Apply selective frequency analysis for phase discrimination\n\n## Common Pitfalls\n1. **Zero-phase filtering**: Always use `filtfilt` to avoid phase shifts\n2. **Edge effects**: Filter output is unreliable near edges; consider tapering\n3. **Aliasing**: Ensure filtering below Nyquist frequency (1/2dt)\n4. **Over-filtering**: Risk of distorting phase onset information\n5. **Detrending**: Remove polynomial trends before filtering to prevent ringing\n\n## Performance Tips\n- Use efficient FFT-based filtering for long signals\n- Pre-filter to reduce computation in phase picking\n- Cache envelope computation if using multiple times\n- Use moving median for robust smoothing instead of moving average\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":3,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1929,"cache_read_input_tokens":22235,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1929,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":18},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"09e6d993-23e9-426b-836d-7610873e9d50","uuid":"d21736d4-ee0e-481e-b7d9-eba7d5957d65"}

Filtering (Noise Reduction)\n\n#### Butterworth Filter\nBest for removing low-frequency noise while preserving phase information\n\n```python\nfrom scipy import signal\nimport numpy as np\n\ndef bandpass_filter(data, dt, lowcut, highcut, order=4):\n    \"\"\"\n    Butterworth bandpass filter\n\n    Args:\n        data: Input signal (1D or 2D array)\n        dt: Sampling interval in seconds\n        lowcut: Low cutoff frequency in Hz\n        highcut: High cutoff frequency in Hz\n        order: Filter order\n\n    Returns:\n        filtered: Filtered signal\n    \"\"\"\n    nyquist = 1 / (2 * dt)\n    low = lowcut / nyquist\n    high = highcut / nyquist\n\n    # Ensure valid range\n    low = max(0.001, min(low, 0.99))\n    high = max(low + 0.001, min(high, 0.99))\n\n    b, a = signal.butter(order, [low, high], btype='band')\n\n    if data.ndim == 1:\n        return signal.filtfilt(b, a, data)\n    else:\n        # Apply to each channel\n        filtered = np.zeros_like(data, dtype=float)\n        for i in range(data.shape[1]):\n            filtered[:, i] = signal.filtfilt(b, a, data[:, i])\n        return filtered\n```\n\n#### High-Pass Filter\nFor removing very low frequency components and drift\n\n```python\ndef highpass_filter(data, dt, cutoff, order=4):\n    \"\"\"High-pass filter for drift removal\"\"\"\n    nyquist = 1 / (2 * dt)\n    norm_cutoff = min(cutoff / nyquist, 0.99)\n    b, a = signal.butter(order, norm_cutoff, btype='high')\n\n    if data.ndim == 1:\n        return signal.filtfilt(b, a, data)\n    else:\n        filtered = np.zeros_like(data, dtype=float)\n        for i in range(data.shape[1]):\n            filtered[:, i] = signal.filtfilt(b, a, data[:, i])\n        return filtered\n```\n\n### 2. Envelope Computation (Hilbert Transform)\n\n```python\nfrom scipy import signal\nimport numpy as np\n\ndef compute_envelope(data):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute envelope using Hilbert transform\n\n    Analytic signal: z(t) = x(t) + j*H[x(t)]\n    Envelope (magnitude): A(t) = sqrt(x(t)^2 + H[x(t)]^2)\n\n    Args:\n        data: 1D or 2D array\n\n    Returns:\n        envelope: Magnitude of analytic signal\n    \"\"\"\n    if data.ndim == 1:\n        analytic_signal = signal.hilbert(data)\n        return np.abs(analytic_signal)\n    else:\n        envelope = np.zeros_like(data, dtype=float)\n        for i in range(data.shape[1]):\n            analytic_signal = signal.hilbert(data[:, i])\n            envelope[:, i] = np.abs(analytic_signal)\n        return envelope\n```\n\n### 3. Time-Frequency Analysis\n\n#### Short-Time Fourier Transform (STFT)\n```python\ndef compute_stft(data, dt, nperseg=256):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute STFT spectrogram\n\n    Args:\n        data: 1D signal\n        dt: Sampling interval\n        nperseg: Segment length for FFT\n\n    Returns:\n        frequencies: Frequency array\n        times: Time array\n        spectrogram: Power spectrogram (2D)\n    \"\"\"\n    fs = 1 / dt\n    frequencies, times, spectrogram = signal.spectrogram(\n        data, fs=fs, nperseg=nperseg, noverlap=nperseg//2\n    )\n    return frequencies, times, spectrogram\n```\n\n#### Continuous Wavelet Transform (CWT)\nBetter for transient detection (phase onsets)\n\n```python\nfrom scipy import signal\nimport numpy as np\n\ndef compute_cwt(data, dt, scales=None, wavelet='morlet'):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute CWT for transient detection\n\n    Args:\n        data: 1D signal\n        dt: Sampling interval\n        scales: Scales to analyze (default: auto)\n        wavelet: Wavelet type ('morlet', 'mexh', etc.)\n\n    Returns:\n        coefficients: CWT coefficients\n        frequencies: Corresponding frequencies\n    \"\"\"\n    fs = 1 / dt\n    if scales is None:\n        scales = np.arange(1, 128)\n\n    coefficients = signal.cwt(data, signal.morlet2, scales)\n    frequencies = scales / (dt * len(data))\n\n    return coefficients, frequencies\n```\n\n### 4. Data Preprocessing\n\n#### Complete Preprocessing Pipeline\n```python\ndef preprocess_trace(waveform, dt, highpass_freq=0.5, lowpass_freq=20):\n    \"\"\"\n    Complete preprocessing: denoise, filter, normalize\n\n    Args:\n        waveform: 2D array (n_samples, n_channels)\n        dt: Sampling interval\n        highpass_freq: High-pass cutoff in Hz\n        lowpass_freq: Low-pass cutoff in Hz\n\n    Returns:\n        processed: Preprocessed waveform\n    \"\"\"\n    # 1. Demean\n    detrended = waveform - np.mean(waveform, axis=0, keepdims=True)\n\n    # 2. Remove linear trend\n    for i in range(detrended.shape[1]):\n        coeffs = np.polyfit(np.arange(len(detrended)), detrended[:, i], 1)\n        trend = np.polyval(coeffs, np.arange(len(detrended)))\n        detrended[:, i] -= trend\n\n    # 3. Bandpass filter\n    filtered = bandpass_filter(detrended, dt, highpass_freq, lowpass_freq)\n\n    # 4. Normalize per channel\n    normalized = np.zeros_like(filtered, dtype=float)\n    for i in range(filtered.shape[1]):\n        std = np.std(filtered[:, i])\n        if std > 0:\n            normalized[:, i] = filtered[:, i] / std\n\n    return normalized\n```\n\n## Frequency Band Selection by Phase Type\n\n### For P Wave Detection\n- **Primary band**: 1-5 Hz (lower frequencies, first arrival)\n- **Complementary**: 0.5-2 Hz for very regional distances\n- Reasoning: P waves have lower frequency content, reduces S wave interference\n\n### For S Wave Detection\n- **Primary band**: 5-20 Hz (higher frequencies, larger amplitudes)\n- **Complementary**: 3-10 Hz for regional distances\n- Reasoning: S waves contain more energy in higher frequencies\n\n### For Joint Analysis\n- **Broad band**: 0.5-20 Hz for overall energy detection\n- Apply selective frequency analysis for phase discrimination\n\n## Common Pitfalls\n1. **Zero-phase filtering**: Always use `filtfilt` to avoid phase shifts\n2. **Edge effects**: Filter output is unreliable near edges; consider tapering\n3. **Aliasing**: Ensure filtering below Nyquist frequency (1/2dt)\n4. **Over-filtering**: Risk of distorting phase onset information\n5. **Detrending**: Remove polynomial trends before filtering to prevent ringing\n\n## Performance Tips\n- Use efficient FFT-based filtering for long signals\n- Pre-filter to reduce computation in phase picking\n- Cache envelope computation if using multiple times\n- Use moving median for robust smoothing instead of moving average\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

This skill covers ensemble strategies for seismic phase detection.\n\n## Installation\n```bash\npip install numpy scipy pandas scikit-learn\n```\n\n## Ensemble Methods\n\n### 1. Voting Strategy\nCombine picks from multiple methods using consensus voting\n\n```python\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy import stats\n\ndef ensemble_pick_voting(picks_list, dt, tolerance_idx, max_vote=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Ensemble picker using voting\n\n    Args:\n        picks_list: List of picks from different methods (each can be None or int index)\n        dt: Sampling interval\n        tolerance_idx: Tolerance in samples for clustering picks\n        max_vote: If not None, require at least max_vote votes (default: majority)\n\n    Returns:\n        final_pick: Best consensus pick (or None if no consensus)\n    \"\"\"\n    # Filter out None picks\n    valid_picks = [p for p in picks_list if p is not None]\n\n    if len(valid_picks) == 0:\n        return None\n\n    if max_vote is None:\n        max_vote = (len(picks_list) + 1) // 2  # Majority vote\n\n    if len(valid_picks) < max_vote:\n        return None\n\n    # Cluster similar picks\n    valid_picks = sorted(valid_picks)\n    clusters = []\n    current_cluster = [valid_picks[0]]\n\n    for pick in valid_picks[1:]:\n        if pick - current_cluster[-1] <= tolerance_idx:\n            current_cluster.append(pick)\n        else:\n            clusters.append(current_cluster)\n            current_cluster = [pick]\n    clusters.append(current_cluster)\n\n    # Find cluster with most votes\n    best_cluster = max(clusters, key=len)\n\n    if len(best_cluster) >= max_vote:\n        return int(np.median(best_cluster))\n    return None\n```\n\n### 2. Score-Based Weighting\nWeight picks based on detection confidence\n\n```python\ndef ensemble_pick_weighted(picks_scores, dt, tolerance_idx):\n    \"\"\"\n    Ensemble with confidence weighting\n\n    Args:\n        picks_scores: List of tuples (pick_idx, confidence_score)\n                      where score is 0-1 (1 = high confidence)\n        tolerance_idx: Tolerance for clustering\n\n    Returns:\n        final_pick: Weighted best pick\n    \"\"\"\n    picks_scores = [(p, s) for p, s in picks_scores if p is not None]\n\n    if not picks_scores:\n        return None\n\n    # Sort by index\n    picks_scores = sorted(picks_scores, key=lambda x: x[0])\n\n    # Cluster picks\n    clusters = []\n    current_cluster = [picks_scores[0]]\n\n    for pick, score in picks_scores[1:]:\n        if pick - current_cluster[-1][0] <= tolerance_idx:\n            current_cluster.append((pick, score))\n        else:\n            clusters.append(current_cluster)\n            current_cluster = [(pick, score)]\n    clusters.append(current_cluster)\n\n    # Score each cluster by sum of confidences\n    best_cluster = max(clusters, key=lambda c: sum(s for _, s in c))\n\n    # Return weighted median\n    indices = [p for p, _ in best_cluster]\n    weights = np.array([s for _, s in best_cluster])\n    weights /= weights.sum()\n\n    return int(np.average(indices, weights=weights))\n```\n\n### 3. Phase-Specific Methods\n\n```python\ndef pick_p_wave(waveform, dt, channels):\n    \"\"\"\n    Multi-method P wave picker\n\n    Args:\n        waveform: 2D array (n_samples, n_channels)\n        dt: Sampling interval\n        channels: List of channel names\n\n    Returns:\n        p_idx: Best P wave pick\n        confidence: Confidence score (0-1)\n    \"\"\"\n    picks = []\n\n    # Ensemble\n    tolerance_idx = int(0.3 / dt)  # 300ms tolerance for P wave\n    p_idx = ensemble_pick_voting(picks, dt, tolerance_idx, max_vote=2)\n\n    if p_idx is None:\n        # Fallback to earliest pick\n        valid_picks = [p for p in picks if p is not None]\n        if valid_picks:\n            p_idx = min(valid_picks)\n        else:\n            p_idx = None\n\n    confidence = len([p for p in picks if p is not None]) / len(picks) if p_idx else 0\n    return p_idx, confidence\n\n\ndef pick_s_wave(waveform, dt, channels, p_idx=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Multi-method S wave picker\n\n    Args:\n        waveform: 2D array\n        dt: Sampling interval\n        channels: List of channel names\n        p_idx: P wave pick index (to constrain S search)\n\n    Returns:\n        s_idx: Best S wave pick\n        confidence: Confidence score\n    \"\"\"\n    picks = []\n\n    # Define search window: S should be 1.2-2.5× time of P\n    if p_idx is not None:\n        search_start = int(p_idx + 0.5 / dt)  # At least 0.5s after P\n        search_end = min(len(waveform), int(p_idx + 60 / dt))  # Up to 60s after P\n    else:\n        search_start = int(2 / dt)  # 2 seconds minimum\n        search_end = len(waveform)\n\n    waveform_search = waveform[search_start:search_end]\n\n    if len(waveform_search) == 0:\n        return None, 0\n\n    # Method 1: High-frequency picker (S waves in 5-20 Hz)\n    from scipy import signal\n    energy = np.sqrt(np.sum(waveform_search ** 2, axis=1))\n    b, a = signal.butter(4, [5/(1/(2*dt)), 20/(1/(2*dt))], btype='band')\n    hf_data = signal.filtfilt(b, a, energy)\n    pick1 = energy_picker(hf_data, dt, window_size=1.5, percentile=70)\n    if pick1 is not None:\n        pick1 += search_start\n    picks.append(pick1)\n\n    # Method 2: Envelope-based picker on horizontal components\n    if 'N' in channels and 'E' in channels:\n        n_idx = channels.index('N')\n        e_idx = channels.index('E')\n        horiz_energy = np.sqrt(waveform_search[:, n_idx]**2 + waveform_search[:, e_idx]**2)\n        pick2 = energy_picker(horiz_energy, dt, window_size=1.0, percentile=75)\n        if pick2 is not None:\n            pick2 += search_start\n        picks.append(pick2)\n    else:\n        picks.append(None)\n\n    # Method 3: STA/LTA on combined energy\n    pick3 = sta_lta_picker(energy, dt, sta_window=1.5, lta_window=25.0, threshold=2.0)\n    if pick3 is not None:\n        pick3 += search_start\n    picks.append(pick3)\n\n    # Ensemble\n    tolerance_idx = int(0.5 / dt)  # 500ms tolerance for S wave\n    s_idx = ensemble_pick_voting(picks, dt, tolerance_idx, max_vote=2)\n\n    if s_idx is None:\n        valid_picks = [p for p in picks if p is not None]\n        if valid_picks:\n            # S comes after P\n        else:\n            s_idx = None\n\n    confidence = len([p for p in picks if p is not None]) / len(picks) if s_idx else 0\n    return s_idx, confidence\n```\n\n## Quality Control and Validation\n\n```python\ndef validate_picks(p_idx, s_idx, dt, min_p_s_separation=0.5):\n    \"\"\"\n    Validate P and S picks for physical consistency\n\n    Args:\n        p_idx: P wave pick index\n        s_idx: S wave pick index\n        dt: Sampling interval\n        min_p_s_separation: Minimum separation in seconds (default 0.5s)\n\n    Returns:\n        is_valid: Boolean indicating valid pick pair\n    \"\"\"\n    if p_idx is None or s_idx is None:\n        return p_idx is not None or s_idx is not None\n\n    # True positives\n    for gt in ground_truth:\n        for pred in predicted:\n            if abs(pred - gt) <= tolerance_idx:\n                tp += 1\n                break\n\n    fp = len(predicted) - tp\n    fn = len(ground_truth) - tp\n\n    precision = tp / (tp + fp) if (tp + fp) > 0 else 0\n    recall = tp / (tp + fn) if (tp + fn) > 0 else 0\n    f1 = 2 * (precision * recall) / (precision + recall) if (precision + recall) > 0 else 0\n\n    return precision, recall, f1\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n1. Choose pick closest to expected S location\n            if p_idx is not None:\n                expected_s = p_idx + int(p_idx / 3)  # Rough v_p/v_s estimate\n                s_idx = min(valid_picks, key=lambda x: abs(x - expected_s))\n            else:\n                s_idx = max(valid_picks)  # S comes after P\n        else:\n            s_idx = None\n\n    confidence = len([p for p in picks if p is not None]) / len(picks) if s_idx else 0\n    return s_idx, confidence\n```\n\n## Quality Control and Validation\n\n```python\ndef validate_picks(p_idx, s_idx, dt, min_p_s_separation=0.5):\n    \"\"\"\n    Validate P and S picks for physical consistency\n\n    Args:\n        p_idx: P wave pick index\n        s_idx: S wave pick index\n        dt: Sampling interval\n        min_p_s_separation: Minimum separation in seconds (default 0.5s)\n\n    Returns:\n        is_valid: Boolean indicating valid pick pair\n    \"\"\"\n    if p_idx is None or s_idx is None:\n        return p_idx is not None or s_idx is not None\n\n    # S should always come after P\n    if s_idx <= p_idx:\n        return False\n\n    # Check minimum separation (physical constraint)\n    separation = (s_idx - p_idx) * dt\n    if separation < min_p_s_separation:\n        return False\n\n    # Check reasonable P-S interval (1-60 seconds for local to regional)\n    if separation > 120:\n        return False\n\n    return True\n```\n\n## Performance Metrics\n\n```python\ndef compute_f1_metrics(predicted, ground_truth, dt, tolerance_s=0.1):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute F1 score for phase picks\n\n    Args:\n        predicted: List of predicted pick indices\n        ground_truth: List of ground truth pick indices\n        dt: Sampling interval\n        tolerance_s: Tolerance in seconds\n\n    Returns:\n        precision, recall, f1: Performance metrics\n    \"\"\"\n    tolerance_idx = int(tolerance_s / dt)\n\n    tp = 0  # True positives\n    for gt in ground_truth:\n        for pred in predicted:\n            if abs(pred - gt) <= tolerance_idx:\n                tp += 1\n                break\n\n    fp = len(predicted) - tp\n    fn = len(ground_truth) - tp\n\n    precision = tp / (tp + fp) if (tp + fp) > 0 else 0\n    recall = tp / (tp + fn) if (tp + fn) > 0 else 0\n    f1 = 2 * (precision * recall) / (precision + recall) if (precision + recall) > 0 else 0\n\n    return precision, recall, f1\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n1. Always combine at least 2-3 independent methods\n2. Use confidence scores to weight method contributions\n3. Apply phase-specific search windows (P early, S later)\n4. Validate picks against physical constraints\n5. Test on subset with ground truth before full deployment\n6. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_01RCxSsTpg5i4bnMVL5RCPuE","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01St5eRyZqfMUQvJS5bHhGnT","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/phase_picker.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nSeismic Phase Picking Solution\nAutomatic P and S wave arrival detection using ensemble methods\n\"\"\"\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport os\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom scipy import signal\nimport pandas as pd\nfrom typing import Optional, Tuple, List\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Signal Processing Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef bandpass_filter(data, dt, lowcut, highcut, order=4):\n    \"\"\"Butterworth bandpass filter\"\"\"\n    nyquist = 1 / (2 * dt)\n    low = lowcut / nyquist\n    high = highcut / nyquist\n\n    low = max(0.001, min(low, 0.99))\n    high = max(low + 0.001, min(high, 0.99))\n\n    b, a = signal.butter(order, [low, high], btype='band')\n\n    if data.ndim == 1:\n        return signal.filtfilt(b, a, data)\n    else:\n        filtered = np.zeros_like(data, dtype=float)\n        for i in range(data.shape[1]):\n            filtered[:, i] = signal.filtfilt(b, a, data[:, i])\n        return filtered\n\n\ndef compute_envelope(data):\n    \"\"\"Compute envelope using Hilbert transform\"\"\"\n    if data.ndim == 1:\n        analytic_signal = signal.hilbert(data)\n        return np.abs(analytic_signal)\n    else:\n        envelope = np.zeros_like(data, dtype=float)\n        for i in range(data.shape[1]):\n            analytic_signal = signal.hilbert(data[:, i])\n            envelope[:, i] = np.abs(analytic_signal)\n        return envelope\n\n\ndef preprocess_trace(waveform, dt, highpass_freq=0.5, lowpass_freq=20):\n    \"\"\"Complete preprocessing: denoise, filter, normalize\"\"\"\n    # 1. Demean\n    detrended = waveform - np.mean(waveform, axis=0, keepdims=True)\n\n    # 2. Remove linear trend\n    for i in range(detrended.shape[1]):\n        coeffs = np.polyfit(np.arange(len(detrended)), detrended[:, i], 1)\n        trend = np.polyval(coeffs, np.arange(len(detrended)))\n        detrended[:, i] -= trend\n\n    # 3. Bandpass filter\n    filtered = bandpass_filter(detrended, dt, highpass_freq, lowpass_freq)\n\n    # 4. Normalize per channel\n    normalized = np.zeros_like(filtered, dtype=float)\n    for i in range(filtered.shape[1]):\n        std = np.std(filtered[:, i])\n        if std > 0:\n            normalized[:, i] = filtered[:, i] / std\n\n    return normalized\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Phase Picking Methods\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef sta_lta_picker(waveform, dt, sta_window=1.0, lta_window=10.0, threshold=2.0):\n    \"\"\"STA/LTA phase picker\"\"\"\n    n_sta = int(sta_window / dt)\n    n_lta = int(lta_window / dt)\n\n    energy = waveform ** 2\n\n    sta = np.convolve(energy, np.ones(n_sta) / n_sta, mode='same')\n    lta = np.convolve(energy, np.ones(n_lta) / n_lta, mode='same')\n\n    lta[lta == 0] = 1\n\n    ratio = sta / lta\n\n    onset_indices = np.where(ratio > threshold)[0]\n\n    if len(onset_indices) > 0:\n        return onset_indices[0]\n    return None\n\n\ndef energy_picker(waveform, dt, window_size=1.0, percentile=90):\n    \"\"\"Energy-based picker using envelope and percentile\"\"\"\n    envelope = np.abs(waveform)\n\n    window = int(window_size / dt)\n    if window < 1:\n        window = 1\n    kernel = np.ones(window) / window\n    smooth_envelope = np.convolve(envelope, kernel, mode='same')\n\n    threshold = np.percentile(smooth_envelope, percentile)\n\n    onset_indices = np.where(smooth_envelope > threshold)[0]\n\n    if len(onset_indices) > 0:\n        return onset_indices[0]\n    return None\n\n\ndef derivative_picker(waveform, dt, threshold_percentile=80):\n    \"\"\"Pick based on maximum derivative (amplitude rate of change)\"\"\"\n    # Smooth first to reduce noise\n    window = int(0.5 / dt)\n    if window < 2:\n        window = 2\n    kernel = np.ones(window) / window\n    smoothed = np.convolve(waveform, kernel, mode='same')\n\n    # Compute derivative\n    derivative = np.abs(np.gradient(smoothed, dt))\n\n    # Find threshold\n    threshold = np.percentile(derivative, threshold_percentile)\n\n    # Find first exceedance\n    onset_indices = np.where(derivative > threshold)[0]\n\n    if len(onset_indices) > 0:\n        return onset_indices[0]\n    return None\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Ensemble Methods\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef ensemble_pick_voting(picks_list, dt, tolerance_idx, min_votes=1):\n    \"\"\"Ensemble picker using voting\"\"\"\n    valid_picks = [p for p in picks_list if p is not None]\n\n    if len(valid_picks) < min_votes:\n        return None\n\n    valid_picks = sorted(valid_picks)\n\n    # Cluster similar picks\n    clusters = []\n    current_cluster = [valid_picks[0]]\n\n    for pick in valid_picks[1:]:\n        if pick - current_cluster[-1] <= tolerance_idx:\n            current_cluster.append(pick)\n        else:\n            clusters.append(current_cluster)\n            current_cluster = [pick]\n    clusters.append(current_cluster)\n\n    # Find largest cluster\n    best_cluster = max(clusters, key=len)\n    return int(np.median(best_cluster))\n\n\ndef pick_p_wave(waveform, dt, channels) -> Tuple[Optional[int], float]:\n    \"\"\"Multi-method P wave picker\"\"\"\n    picks = []\n\n    # Get vertical component if available\n    try:\n        channel_list = channels.split(',')\n        z_idx = None\n        for i, ch in enumerate(channel_list):\n            if 'Z' in ch or 'z' in ch:\n                z_idx = i\n                break\n        if z_idx is None:\n            z_idx = waveform.shape[1] - 1  # Assume last channel is Z\n    except:\n        z_idx = waveform.shape[1] - 1\n\n    # Ensure z_idx is valid\n    if z_idx >= waveform.shape[1]:\n        z_idx = waveform.shape[1] - 1\n\n    z_data = waveform[:, z_idx]\n\n    # Method 1: STA/LTA on vertical component (tight window for early arrival)\n    pick1 = sta_lta_picker(z_data, dt, sta_window=0.5, lta_window=15.0, threshold=2.0)\n    picks.append(pick1)\n\n    # Method 2: Energy picker on combined channels\n    energy = np.sqrt(np.sum(waveform ** 2, axis=1))\n    pick2 = energy_picker(energy, dt, window_size=1.5, percentile=75)\n    picks.append(pick2)\n\n    # Method 3: Low-frequency picker (P waves in 0.5-5 Hz)\n    try:\n        lf_data = bandpass_filter(z_data, dt, 0.5, 5.0, order=3)\n        pick3 = sta_lta_picker(lf_data, dt, sta_window=1.0, lta_window=20.0, threshold=1.8)\n        picks.append(pick3)\n    except:\n        picks.append(None)\n\n    # Method 4: Derivative-based picker\n    try:\n        pick4 = derivative_picker(energy, dt, threshold_percentile=75)\n        picks.append(pick4)\n    except:\n        picks.append(None)\n\n    # Ensemble - be permissive for P waves\n    tolerance_idx = int(0.5 / dt)\n    p_idx = ensemble_pick_voting(picks, dt, tolerance_idx, min_votes=1)\n\n    if p_idx is None:\n        # Fallback to earliest pick\n        valid_picks = [p for p in picks if p is not None]\n        if valid_picks:\n            p_idx = min(valid_picks)\n\n    confidence = len([p for p in picks if p is not None]) / len(picks) if p_idx else 0\n    return p_idx, confidence\n\n\ndef pick_s_wave(waveform, dt, channels, p_idx=None) -> Tuple[Optional[int], float]:\n    \"\"\"Multi-method S wave picker\"\"\"\n    picks = []\n\n    # Define search window: S should come well after P\n    if p_idx is not None:\n        search_start = int(p_idx + 0.5 / dt)  # At least 0.5s after P\n        search_end = min(len(waveform), int(p_idx + 90 / dt))  # Up to 90s after P\n    else:\n        search_start = int(2 / dt)  # 2 seconds minimum\n        search_end = len(waveform)\n\n    if search_start >= search_end or search_start >= len(waveform):\n        return None, 0\n\n    waveform_search = waveform[search_start:search_end]\n\n    # Get horizontal components if available\n    try:\n        channel_list = channels.split(',')\n        n_idx = None\n        e_idx = None\n        z_idx = None\n        for i, ch in enumerate(channel_list):\n            if 'N' in ch or 'n' in ch:\n                n_idx = i\n            elif 'E' in ch or 'e' in ch:\n                e_idx = i\n            elif 'Z' in ch or 'z' in ch:\n                z_idx = i\n\n        # Default assignments\n        if z_idx is None:\n            z_idx = waveform.shape[1] - 1\n        if n_idx is None:\n            n_idx = 0\n        if e_idx is None:\n            e_idx = 1\n    except:\n        z_idx = 2\n        n_idx = 0\n        e_idx = 1\n\n    # Demean\n    detrended = waveform - np.mean(waveform, axis=0, keepdims=True)\n\n    # 2. Remove linear trend\n    for i in range(detrended.shape[1]):\n        coeffs = np.polyfit(np.arange(len(detrended)), detrended[:, i], 1)\n        trend = np.polyval(coeffs, np.arange(len(detrended)))\n        detrended[:, i] -= trend\n\n    # 3. Bandpass filter\n    filtered = bandpass_filter(detrended, dt, highpass_freq, lowpass_freq)\n\n    # 4. Normalize per channel\n    normalized = np.zeros_like(filtered, dtype=float)\n    for i in range(filtered.shape[1]):\n        std = np.std(filtered[:, i])\n        if std > 0:\n            normalized[:, i] = filtered[:, i] / std\n\n    return normalized\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Phase Picking Methods\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef sta_lta_picker(waveform, dt, sta_window=1.0, lta_window=10.0, threshold=2.0):\n    \"\"\"STA/LTA phase picker\"\"\"\n    n_sta = int(sta_window / dt)\n    n_lta = int(lta_window / dt)\n\n    energy = waveform ** 2\n\n    sta = np.convolve(energy, np.ones(n_sta) / n_sta, mode='same')\n    lta = np.convolve(energy, np.ones(n_lta) / n_lta, mode='same')\n\n    lta[lta == 0] = 1\n\n    ratio = sta / lta\n\n    onset_indices = np.where(ratio > threshold)[0]\n\n    if len(onset_indices) > 0:\n        return onset_indices[0]\n    return None\n\n\ndef energy_picker(waveform, dt, window_size=1.0, percentile=90):\n    \"\"\"Energy-based picker using envelope and percentile\"\"\"\n    envelope = np.abs(waveform)\n\n    window = int(window_size / dt)\n    if window < 1:\n        window = 1\n    kernel = np.ones(window) / window\n    smooth_envelope = np.convolve(envelope, kernel, mode='same')\n\n    threshold = np.percentile(smooth_envelope, percentile)\n\n    onset_indices = np.where(smooth_envelope > threshold)[0]\n\n    if len(onset_indices) > 0:\n        return onset_indices[0]\n    return None\n\n\ndef derivative_picker(waveform, dt, threshold_percentile=80):\n    \"\"\"Pick based on maximum derivative (amplitude rate of change)\"\"\"\n    # Smooth first to reduce noise\n    window = int(0.5 / dt)\n    if window < 2:\n        window = 2\n    kernel = np.ones(window) / window\n    smoothed = np.convolve(waveform, kernel, mode='same')\n\n    # Compute derivative\n    derivative = np.abs(np.gradient(smoothed, dt))\n\n    # Find threshold\n    threshold = np.percentile(derivative, threshold_percentile)\n\n    # Find first exceedance\n    onset_indices = np.where(derivative > threshold)[0]\n\n    if len(onset_indices) > 0:\n        return onset_indices[0]\n    return None\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Ensemble Methods\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef ensemble_pick_voting(picks_list, dt, tolerance_idx, min_votes=1):\n    \"\"\"Ensemble picker using voting\"\"\"\n    valid_picks = [p for p in picks_list if p is not None]\n\n    if len(valid_picks) < min_votes:\n        return None\n\n    valid_picks = sorted(valid_picks)\n\n    # Cluster similar picks\n    clusters = []\n    current_cluster = [valid_picks[0]]\n\n    for pick in valid_picks[1:]:\n        if pick - current_cluster[-1] <= tolerance_idx:\n            current_cluster.append(pick)\n        else:\n            clusters.append(current_cluster)\n            current_cluster = [pick]\n    clusters.append(current_cluster)\n\n    # Find largest cluster\n    best_cluster = max(clusters, key=len)\n    return int(np.median(best_cluster))\n\n\ndef pick_p_wave(waveform, dt, channels) -> Tuple[Optional[int], float]:\n    \"\"\"Multi-method P wave picker\"\"\"\n    picks = []\n\n    # Get vertical component if available\n    try:\n        channel_list = channels.split(',')\n        z_idx = None\n        for i, ch in enumerate(channel_list):\n            if 'Z' in ch or 'z' in ch:\n                z_idx = i\n                break\n        if z_idx is None:\n            z_idx = waveform.shape[1] - 1  # Method 3: Low-frequency picker (P waves in 0.5-5 Hz)\n    try:\n        lf_data = bandpass_filter(z_data, dt, 0.5, 5.0, order=3)\n        pick3 = sta_lta_picker(lf_data, dt, sta_window=1.0, lta_window=20.0, threshold=1.8)\n        picks.append(pick3)\n    except:\n        picks.append(None)\n\n    # Method 4: Derivative-based picker\n    try:\n        pick4 = derivative_picker(energy, dt, threshold_percentile=75)\n        picks.append(pick4)\n    except:\n        picks.append(None)\n\n    # Ensemble - be permissive for P waves\n    tolerance_idx = int(0.5 / dt)\n    p_idx = ensemble_pick_voting(picks, dt, tolerance_idx, min_votes=1)\n\n    if p_idx is None:\n        # Default assignments\n        if z_idx is None:\n            z_idx = waveform.shape[1] - 1\n        if n_idx is None:\n            n_idx = 0\n        if e_idx is None:\n            e_idx = 1\n    except:\n        z_idx = 2\n        n_idx = 0\n        e_idx = 1\n\n    # Method 2: Horizontal component picker\n    try:\n        horiz_energy = np.sqrt(waveform_search[:, n_idx]**2 + waveform_search[:, e_idx]**2)\n        pick2 = energy_picker(horiz_energy, dt, window_size=1.0, percentile=75)\n        if pick2 is not None:\n            pick2 += search_start\n        picks.append(pick2)\n    except:\n        picks.append(None)\n\n    # Method 4: Derivative on envelope\n    try:\n        energy_search = np.sqrt(np.sum(waveform_search ** 2, axis=1))\n        pick4 = derivative_picker(energy_search, dt, threshold_percentile=70)\n        if pick4 is not None:\n            pick4 += search_start\n        picks.append(pick4)\n    except:\n        picks.append(None)\n\n    # S should always come after P\n    if s_idx <= p_idx:\n        return False\n\n    # Check minimum separation (physical constraint)\n    separation = (s_idx - p_idx) * dt\n    if separation < min_p_s_separation:\n        return False\n\n    # Check reasonable P-S interval (1-120 seconds for local to regional)\n    if separation > 120:\n        return False\n\n    return True\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Processing\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef process_trace_file(filepath, verbose=False):\n    \"\"\"Process a single trace file and return P and S picks\"\"\"\n    try:\n        data = np.load(filepath)\n        waveform = data['data'].astype(np.float32)\n        dt = float(data['dt'])\n        channels = str(data['channels'])\n\n        # Validate\n        if not validate_picks(p_idx, s_idx, dt):\n            if p_idx is not None and s_idx is not None:\n                # Keep individual picks even if pair is invalid\n                pass\n\n        filename = os.path.basename(filepath)\n        picks = []\n\n        if p_idx is not None:\n            picks.append({\n                'file_name': filename,\n                'phase': 'P',\n                'pick_idx': int(p_idx)\n            })\n\n        if s_idx is not None:\n            picks.append({\n                'file_name': filename,\n                'phase': 'S',\n                'pick_idx': int(s_idx)\n            })\n\n        if verbose:\n            print(f\"Processed {filename}: P={p_idx}, S={s_idx}\")\n\n        return picks\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error processing {filepath}: {e}\")\n        return []\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Process all traces and write results\"\"\"\n    data_dir = '/root/data/'\n    output_file = '/root/results.csv'\n\n    # 1. Demean\n    detrended = waveform - np.mean(waveform, axis=0, keepdims=True)\n\n    # 2. Remove linear trend\n    for i in range(detrended.shape[1]):\n        if len(detrended) > 1:\n            coeffs = np.polyfit(np.arange(len(detrended)), detrended[:, i], 1)\n            trend = np.polyval(coeffs, np.arange(len(detrended)))\n            detrended[:, i] -= trend\n\n    # 3. Cluster similar picks\n    clusters = []\n    current_cluster = [valid_picks[0]]\n\n    for pick in valid_picks[1:]:\n        if pick - current_cluster[-1] <= tolerance_idx:\n            current_cluster.append(pick)\n        else:\n            clusters.append(current_cluster)\n            current_cluster = [pick]\n    clusters.append(current_cluster)\n\n    # 1. Demean\n    detrended = waveform - np.mean(waveform, axis=0, keepdims=True)\n\n    # 2. Remove linear trend\n    for i in range(detrended.shape[1]):\n        if len(detrended) > 1:\n            coeffs = np.polyfit(np.arange(len(detrended)), detrended[:, i], 1)\n            trend = np.polyval(coeffs, np.arange(len(detrended)))\n            detrended[:, i] -= trend\n\n    # 3. Bandpass filter\n    filtered = bandpass_filter(detrended, dt, highpass_freq, lowpass_freq)\n\n    # 4. Compute absolute values (for detection)\n    # Use absolute value since we care about amplitude change, not polarity\n    filtered = np.abs(filtered)\n\n    # 5. Normalize per channel\n    normalized = np.zeros_like(filtered, dtype=float)\n    for i in range(filtered.shape[1]):\n        mean_val = np.mean(filtered[:, i])\n        std = np.std(filtered[:, i])\n        if std > 0:\n            normalized[:, i] = (filtered[:, i] - mean_val) / std\n        else:\n            normalized[:, i] = filtered[:, i]\n\n    return normalized\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Phase Picking Methods\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef sta_lta_picker(waveform, dt, sta_window=1.0, lta_window=10.0, threshold=2.0):\n    \"\"\"STA/LTA phase picker\"\"\"\n    n_sta = int(sta_window / dt)\n    n_lta = int(lta_window / dt)\n\n    energy = waveform ** 2\n\n    sta = np.convolve(energy, np.ones(n_sta) / n_sta, mode='same')\n    lta = np.convolve(energy, np.ones(n_lta) / n_lta, mode='same')\n\n    lta[lta == 0] = 1\n\n    ratio = sta / lta\n\n    onset_indices = np.where(ratio > threshold)[0]\n\n    if len(onset_indices) > 0:\n        return onset_indices[0]\n    return None\n\n\ndef energy_picker(waveform, dt, window_size=1.0, percentile=90, min_duration=5):\n    \"\"\"Energy-based picker using envelope and percentile\"\"\"\n    # Handle both 1D and multi-channel data\n    if waveform.ndim == 1:\n        envelope = np.abs(waveform)\n    else:\n        # Multi-channel: compute quadrature sum\n        envelope = np.sqrt(np.sum(waveform ** 2, axis=1))\n\n    window = int(window_size / dt)\n    if window < 1:\n        window = 1\n    kernel = np.ones(window) / window\n    smooth_envelope = np.convolve(envelope, kernel, mode='same')\n\n    threshold = np.percentile(smooth_envelope, percentile)\n\n    # Find consecutive samples above threshold\n    above_threshold = smooth_envelope > threshold\n\n    # Look for continuous segments\n    onset_indices = np.where(above_threshold)[0]\n\n    if len(onset_indices) > 0:\n        # Return the first index of sustained elevation\n        # (at least min_duration consecutive samples)\n        min_duration_idx = int(min_duration / dt)\n        for i, idx in enumerate(onset_indices):\n            if i + min_duration_idx < len(onset_indices):\n                if onset_indices[i + min_duration_idx] - idx <= min_duration_idx + 1:\n                    return idx\n        # If no sustained elevation found, return earliest\n        return onset_indices[0]\n    return None\n\n\ndef derivative_picker(waveform, dt, threshold_percentile=80):\n    \"\"\"Pick based on maximum derivative (amplitude rate of change)\"\"\"\n    # Smooth first to reduce noise\n    window = int(0.5 / dt)\n    if window < 2:\n        window = 2\n    kernel = np.ones(window) / window\n    smoothed = np.convolve(waveform, kernel, mode='same')\n\n    # Compute derivative\n    derivative = np.abs(np.gradient(smoothed, dt))\n\n    # Find threshold\n    threshold = np.percentile(derivative, threshold_percentile)\n\n    # Find first exceedance\n    onset_indices = np.where(derivative > threshold)[0]\n\n    if len(onset_indices) > 0:\n        return onset_indices[0]\n    return None\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Ensemble Methods\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef ensemble_pick_voting(picks_list, dt, tolerance_idx, min_votes=1):\n    \"\"\"Ensemble picker using voting\"\"\"\n    valid_picks = [p for p in picks_list if p is not None]\n\n    if len(valid_picks) < min_votes:\n        return None\n\n    valid_picks = sorted(valid_picks)\n\n    # Cluster similar picks\n    clusters = []\n    current_cluster = [valid_picks[0]]\n\n    for pick in valid_picks[1:]:\n        if pick - current_cluster[-1] <= tolerance_idx:\n            current_cluster.append(pick)\n        else:\n            clusters.append(current_cluster)\n            current_cluster = [pick]\n    clusters.append(current_cluster)\n\n    # Find largest cluster\n    best_cluster = max(clusters, key=len)\n    return int(np.median(best_cluster))\n\n\ndef pick_p_wave(waveform, dt, channels) -> Tuple[Optional[int], float]:\n    \"\"\"Multi-method P wave picker\"\"\"\n    picks = []\n\n    # Get vertical component if available\n    try:\n        channel_list = channels.split(',')\n        z_idx = None\n        for i, ch in enumerate(channel_list):\n            if 'Z' in ch or 'z' in ch:\n                z_idx = i\n                break\n        if z_idx is None:\n            z_idx = waveform.shape[1] - 1  # Assume last channel is Z\n    except:\n        z_idx = waveform.shape[1] - 1\n\n    # Ensure z_idx is valid\n    if z_idx >= waveform.shape[1]:\n        z_idx = waveform.shape[1] - 1\n\n    z_data = waveform[:, z_idx]\n\n    # Method 1: STA/LTA on vertical component (tight window for early arrival)\n    pick1 = sta_lta_picker(z_data, dt, sta_window=0.5, lta_window=15.0, threshold=2.0)\n    picks.append(pick1)\n\n    # Method 2: Energy picker on combined channels\n    energy = np.sqrt(np.sum(waveform ** 2, axis=1))\n    pick2 = energy_picker(energy, dt, window_size=1.5, percentile=75)\n    picks.append(pick2)\n\n    # Method 3: Low-frequency picker (P waves in 0.5-5 Hz)\n    try:\n        lf_data = bandpass_filter(z_data, dt, 0.5, 5.0, order=3)\n        pick3 = sta_lta_picker(lf_data, dt, sta_window=1.0, lta_window=20.0, threshold=1.8)\n        picks.append(pick3)\n    except:\n        picks.append(None)\n\n    # Method 4: Derivative-based picker\n    try:\n        pick4 = derivative_picker(energy, dt, threshold_percentile=75)\n        picks.append(pick4)\n    except:\n        picks.append(None)\n\n    # Ensemble - be permissive for P waves\n    tolerance_idx = int(0.5 / dt)\n    p_idx = ensemble_pick_voting(picks, dt, tolerance_idx, min_votes=1)\n\n    if p_idx is None:\n        # Fallback to earliest pick\n        valid_picks = [p for p in picks if p is not None]\n        if valid_picks:\n            p_idx = min(valid_picks)\n\n    confidence = len([p for p in picks if p is not None]) / len(picks) if p_idx else 0\n    return p_idx, confidence\n\n\ndef pick_s_wave(waveform, dt, channels, p_idx=None) -> Tuple[Optional[int], float]:\n    \"\"\"Multi-method S wave picker\"\"\"\n    picks = []\n\n    # Define search window: S should come well after P\n    if p_idx is not None:\n        search_start = int(p_idx + 0.5 / dt)  # At least 0.5s after P\n        search_end = min(len(waveform), int(p_idx + 90 / dt))  # Up to 90s after P\n    else:\n        search_start = int(2 / dt)  # 2 seconds minimum\n        search_end = len(waveform)\n\n    if search_start >= search_end or search_start >= len(waveform):\n        return None, 0\n\n    waveform_search = waveform[search_start:search_end]\n\n    # ============================================================================\n# Signal Processing Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef bandpass_filter(data, dt, lowcut, highcut, order=4):\n    \"\"\"Butterworth bandpass filter\"\"\"\n    nyquist = 1 / (2 * dt)\n    low = lowcut / nyquist\n    high = highcut / nyquist\n\n    low = max(0.001, min(low, 0.99))\n    high = max(low + 0.001, min(high, 0.99))\n\n    b, a = signal.butter(order, [low, high], btype='band')\n\n    if data.ndim == 1:\n        return signal.filtfilt(b, a, data)\n    else:\n        filtered = np.zeros_like(data, dtype=float)\n        for i in range(data.shape[1]):\n            filtered[:, i] = signal.filtfilt(b, a, data[:, i])\n        return filtered\n\n\ndef compute_envelope(data):\n    \"\"\"Compute envelope using Hilbert transform\"\"\"\n    if data.ndim == 1:\n        analytic_signal = signal.hilbert(data)\n        return np.abs(analytic_signal)\n    else:\n        envelope = np.zeros_like(data, dtype=float)\n        for i in range(data.shape[1]):\n            analytic_signal = signal.hilbert(data[:, i])\n            envelope[:, i] = np.abs(analytic_signal)\n        return envelope\n\n\ndef preprocess_trace(waveform, dt, highpass_freq=0.5, lowpass_freq=20):\n    \"\"\"Complete preprocessing: denoise, filter, normalize\"\"\"\n    # 1. Demean\n    detrended = waveform - np.mean(waveform, axis=0, keepdims=True)\n\n    # 2. Remove linear trend\n    for i in range(detrended.shape[1]):\n        if len(detrended) > 1:\n            coeffs = np.polyfit(np.arange(len(detrended)), detrended[:, i], 1)\n            trend = np.polyval(coeffs, np.arange(len(detrended)))\n            detrended[:, i] -= trend\n\n    # 3. Bandpass filter\n    filtered = bandpass_filter(detrended, dt, highpass_freq, lowpass_freq)\n\n    # 4. Compute absolute values (for detection)\n    # Use absolute value since we care about amplitude change, not polarity\n    filtered = np.abs(filtered)\n\n    # 5. Normalize per channel\n    normalized = np.zeros_like(filtered, dtype=float)\n    for i in range(filtered.shape[1]):\n        mean_val = np.mean(filtered[:, i])\n        std = np.std(filtered[:, i])\n        if std > 0:\n            normalized[:, i] = (filtered[:, i] - mean_val) / std\n        else:\n            normalized[:, i] = filtered[:, i]\n\n    return normalized\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Phase Picking Methods\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef sta_lta_picker(waveform, dt, sta_window=1.0, lta_window=10.0, threshold=2.0):\n    \"\"\"STA/LTA phase picker\"\"\"\n    n_sta = int(sta_window / dt)\n    n_lta = int(lta_window / dt)\n\n    energy = waveform ** 2\n\n    sta = np.convolve(energy, np.ones(n_sta) / n_sta, mode='same')\n    lta = np.convolve(energy, np.ones(n_lta) / n_lta, mode='same')\n\n    lta[lta == 0] = 1\n\n    ratio = sta / lta\n\n    onset_indices = np.where(ratio > threshold)[0]\n\n    if len(onset_indices) > 0:\n        return onset_indices[0]\n    return None\n\n\ndef energy_picker(waveform, dt, window_size=1.0, percentile=90, min_duration=5):\n    \"\"\"Energy-based picker using envelope and percentile\"\"\"\n    # Handle both 1D and multi-channel data\n    if waveform.ndim == 1:\n        envelope = np.abs(waveform)\n    else:\n        # Multi-channel: compute quadrature sum\n        envelope = np.sqrt(np.sum(waveform ** 2, axis=1))\n\n    window = int(window_size / dt)\n    if window < 1:\n        window = 1\n    kernel = np.ones(window) / window\n    smooth_envelope = np.convolve(envelope, kernel, mode='same')\n\n    threshold = np.percentile(smooth_envelope, percentile)\n\n    # Find consecutive samples above threshold\n    above_threshold = smooth_envelope > threshold\n\n    # Look for continuous segments\n    onset_indices = np.where(above_threshold)[0]\n\n    if len(onset_indices) > 0:\n        # Return the first index of sustained elevation\n        # (at least min_duration consecutive samples)\n        min_duration_idx = int(min_duration / dt)\n        for i, idx in enumerate(onset_indices):\n            if i + min_duration_idx < len(onset_indices):\n                if onset_indices[i + min_duration_idx] - idx <= min_duration_idx + 1:\n                    return idx\n        # If no sustained elevation found, return earliest\n        return onset_indices[0]\n    return None\n\n\ndef derivative_picker(waveform, dt, threshold_percentile=80):\n    \"\"\"Pick based on maximum derivative (amplitude rate of change)\"\"\"\n    # Smooth first to reduce noise\n    window = int(0.5 / dt)\n    if window < 2:\n        window = 2\n    kernel = np.ones(window) / window\n    smoothed = np.convolve(waveform, kernel, mode='same')\n\n    # Compute derivative\n    derivative = np.abs(np.gradient(smoothed, dt))\n\n    # Find threshold\n    threshold = np.percentile(derivative, threshold_percentile)\n\n    # Find first exceedance\n    onset_indices = np.where(derivative > threshold)[0]\n\n    if len(onset_indices) > 0:\n        return onset_indices[0]\n    return None\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Ensemble Methods\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef ensemble_pick_voting(picks_list, dt, tolerance_idx, min_votes=1):\n    \"\"\"Ensemble picker using voting\"\"\"\n    valid_picks = [p for p in picks_list if p is not None]\n\n    if len(valid_picks) < min_votes:\n        return None\n\n    valid_picks = sorted(valid_picks)\n\n    # Cluster similar picks\n    clusters = []\n    current_cluster = [valid_picks[0]]\n\n    for pick in valid_picks[1:]:\n        if pick - current_cluster[-1] <= tolerance_idx:\n            current_cluster.append(pick)\n        else:\n            clusters.append(current_cluster)\n            current_cluster = [pick]\n    clusters.append(current_cluster)\n\n    # Find largest cluster\n    best_cluster = max(clusters, key=len)\n    return int(np.median(best_cluster))\n\n\ndef pick_p_wave(waveform, dt, channels) -> Tuple[Optional[int], float]:\n    \"\"\"Multi-method P wave picker\"\"\"\n    picks = []\n\n    # Assume last channel is Z\n    except:\n        z_idx = waveform.shape[1] - 1\n\n    # Ensure z_idx is valid\n    if z_idx >= waveform.shape[1]:\n        z_idx = waveform.shape[1] - 1\n\n    z_data = waveform[:, z_idx]\n\n    # Ensemble - require at least 1 method to agree\n    tolerance_idx = int(1.0 / dt)  # 1 second tolerance for P wave\n    p_idx = ensemble_pick_voting(picks, dt, tolerance_idx, min_votes=1)\n\n    if p_idx is None:\n        # S should always come after P\n    if s_idx <= p_idx:\n        return False\n\n    # Check minimum separation (physical constraint)\n    separation = (s_idx - p_idx) * dt\n    if separation < min_p_s_separation:\n        return False\n\n    # Check reasonable P-S interval (1-120 seconds for local to regional)\n    if separation > 120:\n        return False\n\n    return True\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Processing\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef process_trace_file(filepath, verbose=False):\n    \"\"\"Process a single trace file and return P and S picks\"\"\"\n    try:\n        data = np.load(filepath)\n        waveform = data['data'].astype(np.float32)\n        dt = float(data['dt'])\n        channels = str(data['channels'])\n\n        # Validate\n        if not validate_picks(p_idx, s_idx, dt):\n            if p_idx is not None and s_idx is not None:\n                # Keep individual picks even if pair is invalid\n                pass\n\n        filename = os.path.basename(filepath)\n        picks = []\n\n        if p_idx is not None:\n            picks.append({\n                'file_name': filename,\n                'phase': 'P',\n                'pick_idx': int(p_idx)\n            })\n\n        if s_idx is not None:\n            picks.append({\n                'file_name': filename,\n                'phase': 'S',\n                'pick_idx': int(s_idx)\n            })\n\n        if verbose:\n            print(f\"Processed {filename}: P={p_idx}, S={s_idx}\")\n\n        return picks\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error processing {filepath}: {e}\")\n        return []\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Process all traces and write results\"\"\"\n    data_dir = '/root/data/'\n    output_file = '/root/results.csv'\n\n    # Implementation\n\n### Main Algorithm: `/root/phase_picker.py`\n\n#### Key Features:\n\n1. **Data Preprocessing**:\n   - Demeaning and linear detrending\n   - Bandpass filtering (0.5-20 Hz)\n   - Absolute value computation for phase-independent detection\n   - Per-channel normalization\n\n2. **P Wave Picking** (4 independent methods):\n   - Energy picker on vertical component\n   - Energy picker on combined 3-component data\n   - STA/LTA on vertical component\n   - Derivative-based picker\n   - **Ensemble rule**: Majority voting with 1-second tolerance\n\n3. **S Wave Picking** (4 independent methods):\n   - Energy picker on combined channels\n   - High-frequency energy picker (5-20 Hz)\n   - Horizontal component picker\n   - STA/LTA on combined energy\n   - **Ensemble rule**: Majority voting with 1.5-second tolerance\n   - **Search window**: Constrained 0.5-90s after P wave arrival\n\n4. **Physical Validation**:\n   - S must arrive after P (s_idx > p_idx)\n   - Minimum P-S Lower frequency content\n   - Detectable on vertical component primarily\n\n2. **S Waves**:\n   - Slower waves (arrive after P)\n   - Larger amplitudes than P waves\n   - Higher frequency content\n   - More pronounced on horizontal components\n\n3. **Wave Speed Ratio** (v_p/v_s ≈ 1.7):\n   - Used to constrain S wave search window\n   - Expected S arrival ≈ 1.7 × P arrival time\n\n## Processing Pipeline\n\n```\nFor each trace file:\n  1. Pick P wave:\n     - Apply 4 independent methods\n     - Ensemble voting with 1s tolerance\n  4. Pick S wave:\n     - Constrain search to 0.5-90s after P\n     - Apply 4 independent methods\n     - Ensemble voting with 1.5s tolerance\n  5. Validate picks against physical constraints\n  6. Write results to CSV\n```\n\n## Output Format\n\nResults are written to `/root/results.csv` with three columns:\n- `file_name`: Original NPZ filename\n- `phase`: \"P\" or \"S\"\n- `pick_idx`: Sample index of arrival\n\nExample:\n```\nfile_name,phase,pick_idx\nBG.ACR..DP.0215954.npz,P,6\nBG.ACR..DP.0215954.npz,S,67\nBG.ACR..DP.0346833.npz,P,48\n```\n\n## Performance Considerations\n\n### F1 Score Targets:\n- **P waves**: ≥ 0.7\n- **S waves**: ≥ 0.6\n- **Tolerance**: 0.1 seconds (100 samples at 10 Hz = 1000 samples at 100 Hz)\n\n### Ensemble Approach Benefits:\n1. **Robustness**: Multiple independent methods reduce false positives/negatives\n2. **Complementary**: Different methods detect different signal characteristics\n3. **Consensus**: Voting ensures high-confidence picks\n4. **Adaptability**: Parameter tuning per phase type (P vs S)\n\n## Data Characteristics\n\n- **100 trace files** in `/root/data/`\n- **All files**: 12,000 samples × 3 channels\n- **Sampling rate**: 0.01s (100 Hz)\n- **Duration**: 120 seconds each\n- **Amplitude**: Very small (1e-14 range) - structure in seismology\n- Channel naming conventions (Z/N/E, H/B, D codes)\n- Time-index conversion using sampling rate\n- Data normalization techniques\n\n**Reusable Code Patterns**:\n```python\n# Load and process any seismic NPZ file\ndata = np.load('trace.npz')\ndt = float(data['dt'])\nwaveform = data['data']\nnormalized = (waveform - waveform.mean(axis=0)) / waveform.std(axis=0)\n```\n\n**Applications Beyond This Task**:\n- Seismic event detection\n- Earthquake magnitude estimation\n- Focal mechanism analysis\n- Any task involving seismic waveforms\n\n---\n\n### Skill 2: Phase Picking Algorithms (`phase-picking-algorithms.md`)\n\n**Purpose**: Core algorithms for identifying seismic wave arrivals\n\n**Key Concepts**:\n- STA/LTA method (real-time detection)\n- Energy-based detection (amplitude tracking)\n- Spectrogram analysis (frequency discrimination)\n- Multi-channel stacking (SNR improvement)\n\n**Physical Understanding**:\n- P waves: Fast, first arrival, sharp onset, lower frequencies\n- S waves: Slower, stronger amplitude, higher frequencies, arrives after P\n\n**Reusable Code Patterns**:\n```python\n# Generic phase picker template\ndef pick_phase(waveform, dt, method='sta_lta'):\n    if method == 'sta_lta':\n        return sta_lta_picker(waveform, dt, sta_window=0.5, ...)\n    elif method == 'energy':\n        return energy_picker(waveform, dt, percentile=80, ...)\n```\n\n**Applications**:\n- Real-time earthquake detection\n- Automated seismic monitoring systems\n- Passive seismic surveys\n- Induced seismicity analysis\n\n---\n\n### Skill 3: Signal Processing for Seismic (`signal-processing-seismic.md`)\n\n**Purpose**: Noise reduction and feature extraction\n\n**Key Techniques**:\n- Butterworth filtering (phase-preserving)\n- Envelope computation (Hilbert transform)\n- Time-frequency analysis (STFT, CWT)\n- Preprocessing pipeline\n\n**Physical Principles**:\n- Frequency band selection per phase type\n- Zero-phase filtering to preserve timing\n- Robust statistics for noise handling\n\n**Reusable Code Patterns**:\n```python\n# Complete preprocessing function\ndef preprocess_seismic(data, dt):\n    detrended = detrend(data)\n    filtered = bandpass_filter(detrended, dt, 0.5, 20)\n    normalized = normalize(filtered)\n    return normalized\n```\n\n**Applications**:\n- Geothermal exploration\n- Volcanic monitoring\n- Seismic hazard assessment\n- Microseismic monitoring (hydraulic fracturing)\n\n---\n\n### Skill 4: Multi-Method Ensemble (`multi-method-ensemble.md`)\n\n**Purpose**: Combine multiple approaches for robust detection\n\n**Key Concepts**:\n- Voting-based consensus\n- Confidence weighting\n- Phase-specific optimizations\n- Physical constraint validation\n\n**Advantages**:\n- Reduces false positives/negatives\n- Works across varying SNR conditions\n- Adaptable to different earthquake types\n- Generalizable to other detection problems\n\n**Reusable Code Patterns**:\n```python\n# Generic ensemble framework\ndef ensemble_pick(picks_list, weights=None, tolerance=1.0):\n    if weights is None:\n        weights = [1/len(picks_list)] * len(picks_list)\n    # ... voting logic ...\n    return consensus_pick\n```\n\n**Applications**:\n- Medical imaging (combining diagnostic methods)\n- Quality control (defect detection)\n- Network intrusion detection\n- Any classification problem requiring high confidence\n\n---\n\n## Integration Into Solution\n\n### How Skills Were Used\n\n1. **Data Loading** → Load 100 trace files and extract components\n2. **Signal Processing** → Apply preprocessing pipeline to each trace\n3. **Phase Picking Algorithms** → Implement 4 independent picking methods\n4. **Ensemble Methods** → Combine 4 methods via voting for final picks\n\n```\nSkill 1: Load Data\n    ↓\nSkill 3: Preprocess (filter, normalize)\n    ↓\nSkill 2: Apply Multiple Picking Algorithms\n    ├─ Method 1 (Energy picker)\n    ├─ Method 2 (STA/LTA)\n    ├─ Method 3 (Derivative picker)\n    └─ Method 4 (Frequency-specific picker)\n    ↓\nSkill 4: Ensemble Voting → Final Pick\n    ↓\nValidate Against Physical Constraints\n    ↓\nWrite CSV Output\n```\n\n### Successful Outcomes\n\n- **100 trace files** processed\n- **200 picks** generated (100 P-waves, 100 S-waves)\n- **3-second processing time** intervals\n\n---\n\n## Extending the Solution\n\n### Adding New Methods\n\nTo add a new picking method, simply:\n\n1. Implement the picker function following the existing pattern\n2. Add it to the methods list in `pick_p_wave()` or `pick_s_wave()`\n3. Ensemble voting automatically incorporates it\n\nExample:\n```python\ndef neural_net_picker(waveform, dt):\n    \"\"\"New ML-based picker\"\"\"\n    model = load_pretrained_model()\n    features = Tasks\n\nThe skills created are applicable to:\n\n1. **Earthquake Detection**: Use Skill 2 + 3 for real-time detection\n2. **Magnitude Estimation**: Use Skill 3 (amplitude analysis)\n3. **Focal Mechanism**: Use Skill 2 (polarization analysis)\n4. **Liquefaction Assessment**: Use Skill 4 (multi-parameter ensemble)\n5. **Aftershock Forecasting**: Use Skill 4 (ensemble prediction)\n\n---\n\n##
